6.112 (07:10, 11-13). t015pf2 at v300, two comparators: EXPLORE (T 0.15) minus std -40 at ply 1 against both
seeds (band -45..-10 met), 5 at ply 2 (null); PROBIT-FIELD minus the std's probit lines -14 at ply 1 and -18 at
ply 2 (-27 against s1, -9 against s2): not confirmed at ply 2 (the v150 -78 was s1's luck and mid-run), and
the deficit sits in the FLAT (F-P -45 / -51 at ply 1, trees -3 / -22) as 6.107 said. Agree on both: explore
closes (T 0.1 stays; lever 3 of 6.96 is done), and the second probit-only seed is not worth running, since
probit-only is not better anywhere and "slightly worse against equal" changes no decision; the mixed
standard stands, and the ladder's probit-only top-12 is selection plus shared-field strength at ply 1 with a
flat that generalises worse (6.96 D, 6.107), closed.
Why T 0.15 costs the LR recipes 60-110 and the probit ones ~10: hotter play labels positions with noisier
outcomes, and the two learners absorb label noise differently. The probit's ADF update is tempered by the
latent's noise (beta) and its variances shrink with evidence, so a noisier label moves a converged weight
little; AdaGrad LR with the base rate 0.05 takes each label at face value and, as lr-settings-2 showed, its
late steps are already on the large side, so extra label noise becomes weight noise; the LR trees pay most
because their leaves see a third of the stream each and average less of it out. It is the lrdecay story
seen from the other side: LR's steps are not too slow, and anything that adds label noise finds them too
large; probit's dynamics are the right ones for a self-play stream. For the record it also says the
exploration dose for a mixed field is set by its LR lines, and a probit-only field could run hotter without
cost, which no decision needs now.

6.113 (07:30, 11-13). labels-p1 extended (s1 v500, s2 v408): search minus result labels pooled 48.5 at ply 1
and 74.5 at ply 2 on the FP face (82 on the full panel), inside the v500 prediction (40-80) and now positive at
ply 1 as well: the result arm's late drift is what the v400 ply-1 cell (-12) was. Search labels are the first
lever since the trees' ply-1 partition to give 50-80 against strangers, and they give it at the plies the
goal cares about. Trees and DAGs have never trained on search labels (train bails for other kinds).
The hypothesis (labels as what lets trees pull ahead at ply 2+) is worth a build, with a clear reason and a
clear risk. The reason: the ply-2-trained tree's deficit is lag (6.44-6.46), its leaves on shares of a moving
stream; a search label is a less noisy target per position (the search resolves the short tactics that the
result label carries as noise), so each leaf's share carries more signal per example, and a less noisy target
is exactly what a learner on a third of the data needs; the flat gained 60-80 from it with all the data, a leaf
with a third should gain at least as much per example. The risk: the label is the TREE'S OWN depth-2 search,
so a tree bootstraps on its own partition's errors (a wrong leaf labels its neighbours' positions), where the
flat's self-labelling is smoother; the fix if it bites is --label-model with the field's flat as the fixed
labeller, which is zero knowledge (another self-play model's search) and a one-flag change already in the
engine's design ("search with this fixed model instead of the model being trained").
Build: tree and DAG training on search labels, the label computed per position by the trainee's own depth-2

labels with --label-model = the field's F-P labels line (fixed labeller); 8 entries, 1,584 games, self-games,

@299 at plies 2 and 3, v150, v300, final; verdicts on the TR face for tree-against-tree cells and both faces
for tree-against-flat. Bands (ply 2 / ply 3): tree-labels minus tree-result 40-100 / 30-80 (at least the flat's
gain); tree-labels minus F-P-labels -20 to 40 / -20 to 40 (the goal's cell: a tree ahead of the labelled flat
at ply 2 would be the first model past the parameter-limited ones at the goal's ply); fixed-labeller minus own-

Rules: tree-labels 30 or more over F-P-labels at ply 2 and not below at ply 3 says the capacity converts the
target and the standard's trees take search labels (then the growing tree and the ply-3 twin); tree-labels
within 15 of F-P-labels says labels lift trees and flats alike and the tie stands.
Rank by expected gain per CPU-h, today: 1. levers-p2 (running; labels on the ply-2 flat; nothing to spend). 2.
THIS (labels-tree-p2; the only live route to a model past the flats at ply 2; ~14 CPU-h plus a build). 3. The

6.114 (07:50, 11-13). residual-p2 at v300: residual minus capped -40 [-65, -14] at ply 2 (v150 -57), -21 at ply 1
(v150 -50); residual minus F-P 18; in-run the residual lines still crown (v260, v280) while the capped ones
stalled at v136-v144. On the line of the 6.107 rule (-40 closes). Ruling: let the final decide, because the
trajectory is the mechanism's own prediction (the chase eases as the field slows: -57 to -40 at ply 2, -50 to
-21 at ply 1) and the residual lines are the ones still moving. Final rule: residual minus capped at ply 2
within 15 at the final (and not below -30 at ply 1) REOPENS residual leaves as "a slower start to the same
place", worth the shrunk-delta variant (a slow delta chases less) in one ply-2 field; -25 or worse CLOSES them
(the chase does not resolve within a run's length); between, closed as a strength lever with the note that it
learns on past the capped tree, which the library may want but the goal does not. Either way it does not
beat the flat (18 over F-P at ply 2 against the capped tree's 58), so it is not the route to the goal; the
tree-labels build (6.113) is.

On pairs3's plan: (1) add the ply-3 (off-ply) read cell to the two ply-1 runs' reads, since a pairs model's
cost one ply off its training is now the lever's defining number and must be read at the same time as any
gain; (2) demote pairs below --label-depth 3 in the ranking: labels have given 50-75 three times at the goal's
plies and pairs have given 47 once, lost it, and cost 60-95 off-ply; (3) do NOT replace the pairs3 runs by a
pairs3-plus-labels combination: the statistic's repair must be read alone first (6.111's clean-list test), and

ply 1 is 30 or more AND ply 3 is not below -30, i.e. only if the repaired pairs carry structure that holds off-
